#### 重要提示

公司董事会及董事、高级管理人员保证本报告及摘要所载资料不存在任何虚假记载、误导性陈述或者重大遗漏，并对其内容的真实性、准确性和完整性承担个别及连带责任。

公司年度财务报告已经中兴华会计师事务所（特殊普通合伙）根据财政部颁布的《企业会计准则（2006）》审计，并出具了标准无保留意见的审计报告。

本年度报告及摘要中比较列报的以前年度财务数据已经中兴华会计师事务所（特殊普通合伙）根据财政部颁布的《企业会计准则（2006）》进行审计调整。

本年度报告中的财务数据为合并报表经审计后的数据，资本相关数据及主要经营指标按照监管要求合并报表口径计算得出。

公司董事长岳隆杰以及财务负责人刘海新，保证年度报告中财务报告的真实、完整。

#### IMPORTANT NOTES

The Board of Directors, the directors and senior management of the Company undertake that there is no misrepresentation, misleading statement, or material omission in the Report and its summary, and accept joint and several liabilities for the authenticity, accuracy and completeness of the contents of the Report.

The annual financial report of the Company has been audited by Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP according to the Accounting Standards for Business Enterprises (2006) promulgated by the Ministry of Finance and a standard unqualified auditor's opinion has been issued.

The financial data of previous years listed in this annual report and its summary for comparison have been audited and adjusted by Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP according to the Accounting Standards for Business Enterprises (2006) promulgated by the Ministry of Finance.

The financial data listed in this annual report is the audited data of consolidated statements and capital related data has been calculated upon the standard of consolidated statements in accordance with the regulatory requirements.

Chairman Yue Longjie and Finance Director Liu Haixin confirm the authenticity and completeness of the financial statements in the Annual Report.

Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.

## 九、公司各类风险和风险管理情况

公司全面风险管理体系由董事会、监事会、高级管理层、全面风险管理部门、风险管理主控部门、风险管理责任部门、内审部门及各分支机构构成，并建立了包括政策制度、管理办法、操作细则、规范性文件在内的四层级制度体系。公司董事会负责建立和保持有效的全面风险管理体系，承担风险管理的最终责任。高级管理层负责制定风险管理政策和程序，承担全面风险管理的实施责任。高级管理层下设信用风险审查委员会、资产负债管理委员会等专业委员会，并指定各类风险主控部门及管理部门。针对信用风险、市场风险、操作风险等各类风险，公司制定了相应的风险管理政策及程序，建设上线信用风险、操作风险、流动性风险等风险管理系统，明确了各类风险识别、计量、监测、缓释及报告流程，确保各类风险能够准确识别、有效处置。其中，

公司采用权重法计量信用风险加权资产及资本要求，采用标准法计量市场风险加权资产及资本要求，采用基本指标法计量操作风险加权资产及资本要求。2020年，公司严格按照董事会工作部署，坚持以高质量稳健发展为中心，不断健全全面风险管理体系，持续完善风险管理制度流程，升级优化相关风险管理系统，扎实推动风控措施落地落实，实现业务产品全面覆盖、制度流程全程控制、风险防控全员参与，保障了各项业务持续稳健发展。

## (一)信用风险及对策

信用风险是指债务人或交易对手未能履行合同规定的义务或信用质量发生变化，影响金融产品价值，从而给公司造成经济损失的风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强信用风险管理：一是夯实信贷管理基础。制定信贷条线工作指导意见，明确年度工作思路、工作目标及具体措施，修订完善信贷管理基本制度，简化优化各项业务操作流程，全面提升信贷管理工作质效。二是推行信贷精细化管理。制定2020年授信政策指引，全面推行授信项目限时办结、延伸审查和后评价，扎实推动放款审核规范化管理，不断提升分层次贷后管理效能，严防新增信用风险。三是强化信贷管理科技赋能。以对公信贷作业流程为主线，建设上线信贷业务科技赋能项目，涵盖智准营销、智信尽调、智汇风控等五大引擎，不断提升客户经理获客能力、作业标准化和风控水平。四是优化信用风险管理系统。升级优化信用风险管理系统，顺利完成系统框架信息安全升级、评级模型优化、评级流程再造等工作，进一步健全非零售与零售内部评级体系，严把授信准入关口。五是持续开展资产质量攻坚。按照“尊重历史、着眼当前、充分反映、积极处置”原则，制定信用风险处置化解方案和责任划段管理台账，按月倒排工期，积极化解存量，全面提升信贷资产处置质量。

## (二) 市场风险及对策

市场风险是指因市场价格（利率、汇率、股票价格和商品价格）的不利变动，从而使银行表内外业务发生损失的风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强市场风险管理：一是建立指标监测预警机制。健全市场利率信息收集、反馈机制，逐日监控市场信用违约情况，按季监测利率敏感性指标，及时采取措施消除风险隐患。二是不断强化限额管理。制定市场风险偏好策略，明确资金交易限额、债券投资限额、交易性债券止损限额阈值，密切监测各类限额政策执行情况，确保单一限额、日累计限额符合限额管理要求。三是持续加大资金业务风险管控。合理配置短期资产，适当投资货币基金，不断提升短期资金利用率及收益水平，规避市场波动带来的利率风险。建设上线新版资金业务管理系统，全面分析交易品种、投资组合和账户信息，为资金投资提供决策依据。四是加强银行账户利率风险管理。在对国内宏观经济、财政政策进行研究分析基础上，科学研判未来存贷款利率走势，不断完善产品定价管理系统，健全客户定价模型，实现以客户综合价值评价为基础的定价测算。五是有效规范汇率风险管理。制定贸易金融业务定价精细化方案，修

订外汇资金定价管理办法，优化代客结售汇汇率定价机制，并实行外汇交易逐级授权，有效防范汇率风险。

## (三)操作风险及对策

操作风险是指由不完善或有问题的内部控制程序、员工、信息科技系统以及外部事件所造成损失的风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强操作风险管理：一是组织开展“合规文化深化年”。制定“合规文化深化年”实施方案，扎实开展合规规制、合规执行、合规监督、合规整纠等环节建设，并举办合规政策及文化宣讲、合规征文及主题演讲等活动，努力构建“令行禁止、照章办事、知行合一、主动合规”的合规文化。二是持续深化制度流程建设。制定《莱商银行制度管理办法》，建立制度制定、执行、监督、评估、维护的全流程闭环管理机制，有效规范制度建设与管理。同时修订完善内控合规“一图两表”、押品评估管理办法等制度流程，为全员合规操作提供简洁、实用的指导手册。三是全面开展内控检查排查。制定年度内控合规与操作风险检查排查计划，推动完成重点领域检查排查，并对分行开展飞行检查，及时发现和纠正可能存在的操作风险隐患。四是持续强化案件防控管理。全面推行案防工作目标管理责任制，修订完善案件防控工作管理办法、员工违规行为处罚办法等制度，并组织开展员工行为排查和案防知识学习测试等工作，进一步提升全行案件防控管理水平。

## (四)流动性风险及对策

流动性风险是指公司无法以合理成本及时获得充足资金，用于偿付到期债务、履行其他支付义务和满足正常业务开展的其他资金需求的风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强流动性风险管理：一是不断加强流动性监测预警。借助资产负债管理系统，加强流动性比例、流动性缺口率等指标监测预警，及时采取措施消除风险隐患，确保流动性合理充裕。密切关注货币市场资金走势，认真做好月末、节假日、缴税期等时点资金计划与安排，保障特殊时点支付安全。二是调整优化资产负债结构。负债端，通过调整定期存款FTP定价策略、加大存单推广发行力度等措施，不断提高负债来源多元化和稳定性。资产端，积极调整优化投资业务结构，合理配置资产到期期限，持续压降自营投资规模，适当增持国债、政策债等优质资产，实现流动性充足。三是持续深化应急能力建设。按季开展流动性压力测试，科学设置流动性压力测试情景，重点测试存款流失情境下流动性风险承受能力，修正完善流动性应急预案，进一步提升流动性风险识别、监测、预警和处置能力。

## （五）信息科技风险及对策

信息科技风险是指信息科技在银行运用过程中，由于自然因素、人为因素、技术漏洞和管理缺陷产生的操作、法律和声誉等风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强信息科技风险管理：一是

不断完善信息科技治理体系。修订完善信息科技管理委员会工作规则，定期召开信息科技管理委员会会议，并扎实开展信息科技管理制度宣讲，信息科技治理体系不断完善。二是持续加强基础设施建设。制定年度信息科技基础建设规划，顺利完成同城灾备网络优化完善、虚拟化平台版本升级等工作，并组织开展数据中心基础设施隐患排查，不断增强信息科技系统运行稳定性。三是逐步提高网络安全管理能力。组织开展非授权软件风险排查和电子设备漏洞检测等工作，搭建数据信息保护系统和态势感知数据上报平台，不断提升数据信息安全防范能力。四是持续加大信息安全保护力度。印发《信息安全月报》，组织开展客户信息保护专项培训、信息安全专项整治活动、信息科技安全巡查等工作，进一步提升信息安全水平。五是不断增强业务连续性管理能力。修订完善业务连续性管理委员会工作规则和办法，制定年度业务连续性计划，建设完成异地数据级灾备中心，组织开展各类演练，进一步提升全行业务连续性管理能力。

## 十、内部控制评价

公司高度重视内部控制体系建设，严格遵循全覆盖、制衡性、审慎性及相匹配原则，不断健全内控管理架构，持续完善内控管理机制，及时修订内控管理制度流程，并扎实推动各项内控管理措施落地实施。通过对内控体系建设、内控措施实施和内控运行结果进行系统性调查、测试、分析和评估，公司内部控制体系从内部控制环境、风险识别与评估、内部控制措施与执行、内部检查监督、信息交流与反馈等方面，基本涵盖了所有业务、产品、渠道、管理及其关键环节等内控要素，内部控制体系基本健全，流程设置科学合理，在完善公司治理体系、推动全行转型发展、创新创优业务产品、防范化解风险等方面得到了有效执行。

## 十一、内部审计情况

公司内部审计工作由董事会领导、监事长分管，确保了审计的独立性。公司设立了内审部，负责对经营管理、内部控制、风险管理、财务管理、薪酬管理及管理人员履职等事项进行审计，向董事会和下设的审计委员会负责并报告工作，接受监事会监督和指导。

报告期内，公司围绕发展中心和战略部署，严格落实监管要求，实施以风险为导向的审计活动，将业务合规性、风险和内部控制隐患作为审计监督主线和检查重点，完善审计管理机制，加强审计信息化建设，拓展审计监督广度和深度，全面落实年度审计计划，突出对主要分支机构、重点业务领域、操作流程、关键环节及重要岗位人员的审计监督，并加强对审计发现问题整改工作的跟踪与督导，有效发挥内部审计的监督、评价和咨询作用，助推风险管理、内部控制和公司治理水平持续提升，为公司的稳健经营和高质量发展提供有力保障。

## IX. Various Risks and Risk Management of the Company

The Company’s comprehensive risk management system is composed of the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors, senior management, the Comprehensive Risk Management Department, the Main Risk Management Control Department, the Risk Management Responsibility Department, the Internal Audit Department and their branches, and has established a four-tier system including policy system, management measures, operation rules and normative documents. The Board of Directors is responsible for establishing and maintaining an effective and comprehensive risk management system and ultimately for risk management. The senior management is responsible for formulating the risk management policies and procedures and for implementing comprehensive risk management. The senior management has set

up special committees such as the Credit Risk Review Committee and the Assets-Liabilities Management Committee, and also designated the main control departments and management departments of various risks. The company has formulated the corresponding risk management policies and procedures for various risks such as credit risk, market risk and operation risk, built an online management information system for credit risk, operation risk and liquidity risk, and specified various types of risk identification, measurement, monitoring, mitigation and reporting processes to ensure that various risks can be accurately identified and effectively disposed, that is, the Company has taken the weighting method to measure credit risk weighted assets and capital requirements, the standard method to measure market risk weighted assets and capital requirements, and the basic index method to measure operation risk weighted assets and capital requirements. In 2020, in strict accordance with the work arrangement of the Board of Directors, the Company adhered to the high-quality and steady development as her central task, keep improving the comprehensive risk management system and the risk management institutional process flow, upgrade and optimize relevant risk management systems, promote taking risk control measures, basically achieve comprehensive coverage of business product, exercise full control over the system process, and fully participate in risk prevention and control, thus ensuring the sustained and steady development of each business.

#### (i) Credit Risks and Their Countermeasures

Credit risk means the risk of economic loss to the Company caused by influence on the value of financial product resulted from failure to fulfill obligations stipulated in the contract of or change of credit quality of debtors or counterparties. During the reporting period, the Company strengthened credit risk management from the following aspects: first, consolidated the foundation of credit management; formulate guidance on the work of credit line, clarify the annual work considerations, work objectives and specific measures, revise and improve the basic credit management system, simplify and optimize all business operation processes, and comprehensively improve the quality and efficiency of credit management; secondly, implemented meticulous credit management: formulated credit policy guidelines of 2020, fully implemented the regulations of time-limited completion, extended review and post-evaluation of credit projects, solidly promote standardized management of loan review, keep improving the efficiency of post-loan management at different levels, and strictly control new credit risks; thirdly, strengthened the technological empowerment of credit management: taking the corporate credit operation process as the main line, build a technology empowerment project for online credit business, covering five engines, such as intelligent accurate marketing, intelligent due diligence and intelligent risk control, keep improving the customer acquisition skill, operation standardization and risk control of account managers; fourthly, optimized the credit risk management system: upgraded and optimized the credit risk management system, successfully completed information security upgrade of the system framework, rating model optimization, rating process re-engineering and the like work, further improved the non-retail and retail internal rating system, and strictly controlled the credit access threshold; fifthly, continued tackling key problems in asset quality: formulated solutions to credit risk disposition and resolution and a responsibility division management ledger on the principle of "respect for history, focus on the present, fully reflection and actively disposal", back-schedule the time for completion on a monthly basis, actively resolve the stock, and comprehensively improve the disposal quality of credit asset.

#### (ii) Market Risks and Their Countermeasures

Market Risk means the risks of loss to the Bank's business activities on and off the balance sheet due to any adverse change in market price (interest rate, exchange rate, share price and commodity price). During the reporting period, the Company strengthened market risk management from the following aspects: first, established an index monitoring and early warning mechanism: improve the market interest rate information collection and feedback mechanism, monitor market credit default on a daily basis, monitor interest rate sensitivity indicators quarterly, and take timely measures to eliminate potential risk; secondly, continuously strengthen quota management: formulate a market risk preference strategy, specify the threshold of capital trading limit, bond investment limit and tradable bond stop loss limit, closely monitor the implementation of all limit policies, and ensure that the single limit and daily cumulative limit meet the limit management requirement; thirdly, continued increasing risk control of capital business: reasonably allocated short-term assets, appropriately invested in monetary fund, made continuous improvement of short-term fund utilization rate and income level to avoid interest rate risks caused by market fluctuations; built a new online fund business management system to comprehensively analyze transaction varieties, investment portfolios and account information to provide decision-making basis for fund investment; fourthly, strengthened interest rate risk management in bank account: scientifically judged the future trend of deposit and loan interest rate on the basis of research and analysis of domestic macro-economic and fiscal policies of China, made continuous improvement of product pricing management system, improved the customer pricing model, and realized pricing calculation based on customer comprehensive value evaluation; fifthly, effectively regulated exchange rate risk management: made a refined pricing plan for trade finance business, revised the pricing management measures for foreign exchange fund, optimized the exchange rate pricing mechanism for settlement and sale of foreign exchange on behalf of customers, and implemented step-by-step authorization for foreign exchange transactions to effectively prevent exchange rate risk.

#### (iii) Operational Risks and Their Countermeasures

Operational risk is defined as the risk of loss resulting from inadequate or failed internal control processes, personnel and IT systems or from external event. During the reporting period, the Company strengthened operational risk management from the following aspects: first, organized the campaign of "Year of Deepening Compliance Culture": formulated the implementation plan of the "Year of Deepening Compliance Culture", constructed compliance regulation, compliance implementation, compliance supervision, compliance rectification and so on in a down-to-earth manner, and held activities such as compliance policy and culture publicity, solicitation of articles or essays on compliance and keynote speeches to build a compliance culture of "executing orders and proclaim prohibition, acting according to regulations, integrating knowledge and practice, and active compliance with regulations"; secondly, continued deepening construction of institutional processes: formulated the Measures for the System Management of Laishang Bank, established a closed-loop management mechanism for the full process of system formulation, implementation, supervision, evaluation and maintenance, and effectively standardize the system construction and management, revise and improve systems and processes of internal control and compliance such as "one blueprint of planning, one time schedule and one execution program", collateral evaluation management measures and so on to provide concise and practical instructions for all compliance operations; thirdly, launched internal control inspection and investigation in an all-round way: made an annual internal control compliance and operational risk inspection and investigation.

plan, promoted the completion of inspection and investigation in key areas such as enterprise finance, trade finance and retail, and make a spot inspection on branches to discover and correct possible operational risks in time; fourthly, continued strengthening case prevention and control management: fully implemented the target management responsibility system for case prevention, revised and improved management measures for case prevention and control and punishment measures for employee's violation, and organized the investigation on employee's behaviors and study and test of case prevention knowledge so as to further improve the management of case prevention and control throughout the Bank.

#### (iv) Liquidity Risks and Their Countermeasures

The liquidity risk means the risk that a commercial bank is unable to acquire sufficient capital in time with reasonable cost to discharge matured debts, perform other obligations of payment and meet other demand of capital for normal operation of business. During the reporting period, the Company strengthened liquidity risk management from the following aspects: first, continuously strengthened liquidity monitoring and early warning: strengthened the monitoring and early warning of indicators such as liquidity ratio and liquidity gap rate with the help of the asset and liability management system, and took timely measures to eliminate potential risk to ensure reasonable and abundant liquidity, paid close attention to the trend of capital in the monetary market, earnestly planned and arranged capital in the end of each month, holidays, tax payment periods and other time points to ensure the safety of payment at special time points; secondly, adjusted and optimized the structure of assets and liabilities: on the side of liabilities, continuously improved the diversification and stability of liability source by adjusting the FTP pricing strategy of time deposit and increasing the promotion and issuance of certificate of deposit; on the side of assets, actively adjusted and optimized the investment business structure, rationally allocated the maturity of assets, continuously reduced the scale of proprietary investment, and appropriately increased the holding of high-quality assets such as treasury bonds and policy bonds to achieve sufficient liquidity; thirdly, continued deepening construction of emergency response capacity: performed liquidity stress test quarterly, scientifically set liquidity stress test scenarios, focused on test liquidity risk tolerance under deposit loss scenarios, revised and improved liquidity emergency response plans, and further enhanced liquidity risk identification, monitoring, early warning and disposal capabilities.

##### (v) IT Risks and Their Countermeasures

The IT risk means the Bank’s operational, legal and reputational risk arising from natural factors, artificial factors, technology bug and management flaw in the application of IT. During the reporting period, the Company strengthened information technology risk management from the following aspects: first, continuously improved the information technology governance system: revised and improved the rules of procedure of the Information Technology Management Committee, hold regular meetings of the Information Technology Management Committee, and carried out solid publicity on the information technology management system, and continuously improved the information technology governance system; secondly, continued strengthening infrastructure construction: made the annual information technology infrastructure plan, successfully completed the optimization and improvement of local disaster preparedness network, upgraded the version of virtualization platform, etc., and organized the investigation of hidden trouble in data center infrastructure to continuously enhance the operational stability of information technology systems; thirdly, gradually improved the network security management capability: organized risk screening of unauthorized software and vulnerability detection of electronic

equipment, built a data information protection system and a situational awareness data reporting platform, and continuously improved the ability of data information security; fourthly, continued strengthening information security protection: issued the Information Security Monthly Report, organized and delivered special training on customer information protection, special rectification activities on information security, information technology security inspection and the like to further improve information security; fifthly, continuously enhanced business continuity management capabilities: revised and improved the working rules and methods of the Business Continuity Management Committee, made the annual business continuity plan, built and completed the remote data-level disaster recovery center, organized all drills required, and further enhanced the business continuity management capability of the entire bank.

## X. Evaluation of Internal Control

The Company attached great importance to construction of internal control system strictly on the principles of full coverage, checks and balances, prudence and matching, made continuous improvement of internal control management structure and internal control management mechanism, timely revised the internal control management system process, and solidly promoted taking all internal control management measures. Through systematic investigation, test, analysis and evaluation of internal control system construction, internal control measures and internal control operation result, the Company's internal control system, in terms of internal control environment, risk identification and evaluation, internal control measures and implementation, internal inspection and supervision, information exchange and feedback, has basically covered all internal control elements such as business, product, channel, management and their key processes; the internal control system is basically perfected and the process setting is scientific and reasonable, and have been effectively implemented in improving the corporate governance system, promoting the transformation and development of the entire bank, innovating and creating excellent business product, avoiding and resolving risks.

## XI. Internal Audit

The Company’s internal audit is under the leadership of the Board of Directors and in the responsibility of the Chief Supervisor to ensure the independence of the audit. This Bank has set up an internal audit department, which is responsible for auditing operation management, internal control, risk management, financial management, payroll management and performance of duties by management, responsible and reporting to the Board of Directors, subject to the guidance of the Board of Supervisors and inspection, supervision and evaluation of the audit committee;

During the reporting period, focus on the development center and strategic deployment in strict compliance with regulatory requirement, launch risk-oriented audit activities, take business compliance, risk and hidden danger of internal control as the main line of audit and supervision and the focus of inspection, improve the audit management mechanism, strengthen the informatization construction of audit, expand the breadth and depth of audit supervision, fully implement the annual audit plan, highlight the audit supervision on major branches, key business areas, operational processes, key processes and personnel on important positions, and strengthen the tracking and supervision on the rectification of problems found in audit, effectively play the role of supervision, evaluation and consultation of internal audit, promote the continuous improvement of risk management, internal control and corporate governance, and provide a strong guarantee for the steady operation and high-quality development of the Company.

## 四、关联交易情况

根据中国银保监会、企业会计准则关联交易管理的有关规定，公司按不同监管要求对关联方的不同定义，分类识别与确认关联方，定期更新确定关联方名单。

公司与关联方发生关联交易业务均系公司正常经营活动需要，交易条件及定价水平均执行公司业务管理和监管机构的一般规定，不存在优于一般借款人或交易对手的情形，业务程序亦符合监管机构及公司关联交易管理的相关规定。

报告期内，公司的关联交易主要为对关联方提供的表内外信贷业务及与关联方发生的资金业务往来，全部关联交易均正常履约，未对公司的经营成果和财务状况产生负面影响。

word-wrap: break-word;'>葛茂新</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>因特网接入服务业务；对外提供经济技术合作业务；煤炭开采、洗选，道路运输，燃气生产、经营，化工产品（不含化学危险品）销售，石膏开采及石膏制品、许可范围内烧碱、液氯、盐酸、次氯酸钠、聚氯乙烯、三氯氢硅、四氯化硅、液体消毒剂的生产、销售，饮食服务，社区服务，计量授权证书范围内部强制检定。一般经营项目：采矿设备制造、煤炭、建筑材料的销售，设备租赁，农牧养殖，塑料制品、橡胶制品、纺织品、服装、工艺品（不含金饰品）的生产、销售，对所属企业的管理，煤炭开采技术咨询、服务，批准范围内的进出口业务、房屋租赁。</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; (hereinafter referred to as SDHS) and Qilu Transportation Development Group Co., Ltd. and Qilu Transportation Development Group Co., Ltd. (hereinafter referred to as Qilu Transportation) have realized merger and reorganization in the form of consolidation by merger. After the consolidation, Qilu Transportation was dissolved and cancelled, and SDHS continues to exist as a merged company. All assets, liabilities, business, personnel, contracts, qualifications and all other rights and obligations of Qilu Transportation are inherited by SDHS. In addition, Qilu Transportation has finished the industrial and commercial cancellation procedures on November 16, 2020. In view of this, the 600,000,000 shares of our bank previously held by Qilu Transportation were inherited by SDHS.

## IV. The Related Transactions

In accordance with the relevant provisions of China Banking and Insurance Regulatory Commission and the related transaction management of the enterprise accounting standards, the Company, in accordance with different definitions of the related parties based on different regulatory requirements, classified and identified the related parties and regularly updated and identified the list of related parties.

The related transaction business between the Company and related parties is the normal business requirement of the Company, and the general provisions of the Company's business management and regulatory agencies are implemented for the transaction conditions and pricing levels, without any circumstance better than the general borrower or counterparty. The business procedures also comply with the related provisions of the regulatory authorities and the Company's related transaction management.

During the reporting period, the Company's related transactions are mainly in-balance-sheet and off-balance-sheet credit businesses provided for the related party and the capital transactions occurring in the related party. All related transactions are performed normally, without any negative effect on the Company's operating results and financial conditions.

#### (IV) Implementation of Regulatory Provisions

At the end of the reporting period, the maximum credit granting balance for a single affiliated party was RMB 0.5 billion, accounting for 3.5% of the total net capital of the Bank; that for a single related legal person or group client to which the other organization belongs was RMB 1.183 billion, accounting for 8.32% of the total net capital of the Bank; the total credit granting balance for all affiliated parties was RMB 4.061 billion, accounting for 28.57% of the total net capital of the Bank, all of which met the regulatory requirements.

## (六)公司薪酬架构情况

### 1. 薪酬管理架构及决策程序

公司建立了科学的薪酬管理架构，明确规定董事、监事的报酬由股东大会决定，董事会决定高级管理人员的报酬。董事会对公司薪酬管理负最终责任，负责审阅、批准、修订公司薪酬管理制度与政策、薪酬成本与预算等，监督公司薪酬制度实施情况，并定期评估公司薪酬机制的健全性和有效性。董事会下设提名与薪酬考核委员会，负责审议有关薪酬制度、政策及监督工作。高级管理层负责实施董事会薪酬管理方面的决议，组织制定并更新公司薪酬管理制度与政策，管理和控制薪酬预算与成本，审阅、批准员工的定薪、调薪方案、绩效考核方案、员工福利方案等。（公司纳入济南市市属国有企业负责人范围的高级管理人员，其薪酬由济南市国资委牵头考核确定；公司其他高级管理人员的薪酬由董事会和下设的提名与薪酬考核委员会根据董事会制订的薪酬考核办法考核确定。）

### 2. 年度薪酬总额、受益人与结构分布情况

2020年，公司薪酬总额为61575万元，薪酬结构分布如下：

固定薪酬20903万元，占比33.95%；可变薪酬28887万元，占比46.91%；五险一金等福利性收入11785万元。

### 3. 薪酬与业绩衡量、风险调整的标准

公司总行部室员工绩效工资按照《莱商银行总行部室绩效考核管理办法》规定进行考核后兑现，分支行绩效工资按照《莱商银行绩效考核管理办法》进行考核后兑现。

### 4.薪酬延期支付和非现金薪酬情况，包括因故扣回的情况。

公司建立了绩效薪酬的延期支付机制，根据员工不同层级及岗位差异，对各级管理人员以及对风险有重要影响岗位上的员工的绩效薪酬实行延期支付，并设定延期支付时间及支付比例，对延期追索及扣回的情形均做了特别的规定说明。

### 5.薪酬政策

公司薪酬制度遵循“市场导向、岗位导向、绩效导向、能力导向”的付薪理念，于2016年开展了薪酬体系优化设计项目，根据员工岗位职责及标准进行人岗匹配，实行了以岗定薪、按月拨付的固定薪酬制度，将薪酬总额与全行经营利润挂钩，建立自上而下的薪酬总额挂钩经营利润的提取机制，以及自下而上的薪酬总额核算机制，逐步健全全行薪酬总额管控机制。

根据监管要求并结合公司实际，公司制定了《莱商银行薪酬管理办法》《莱商银行绩效薪酬延期支付管理办法》，上述制度已经公司职工代表大会审议通过。

公司薪酬实行统一管理、分级实施的管理体系，并致力于建设科学合理的薪酬管理体系。董事会按照国家有关法律和政策确定公司的薪酬管理制度和政策，并对薪酬管理负最终责任。董事会设立提名与薪酬考核委员会，由独立董事担任主任。高级管理层负责履行薪酬管理职责，总行人力资源部、计划财务部等部门负责执行薪酬政策，内审部负责对薪酬政策执行情况进行监督。

### 6. 年度薪酬方案制定及考核完成情况

公司年度薪酬方案根据人员规模、业务发展情况等制定，2020年度，公司各项经营指标良好，薪酬情况合理的反映了公司业绩及风险管理情况。

7. 超出原定薪酬方案的例外情况

报告期内，公司无超出原定薪酬的例外情况。

## 二、员工情况

报告期末，公司在职员工人数为2326人，员工中本科及以上学历2023人，占比86.97%。

#### (vi) Remunerations Structure of the Company

## 1. Remunerations Management Structure and Decision-making Procedure

The company has established a scientific remuneration management structure, clearly stipulating that the remuneration of directors and supervisors shall be decided by the general meeting of shareholders, and the Board of Directors shall decide the remuneration of senior managers. The Board of Directors is ultimately responsible for the salary management of the Bank, responsible for reviewing, approving, revising the remuneration management system and policies, and remuneration cost and budget of the Bank, supervising the implementation of the Bank's remuneration system, and regularly assessing the soundness and effectiveness of the remuneration mechanism. Under the Board of Directors is the Nomination and Remuneration Assessment Committee, which is responsible for reviewing work relating to the remuneration system, policy and supervision. Senior management is responsible for the implementation of resolutions of the Board of Directors about the remuneration management, organizing to develop and update the remuneration management system and policies, managing and controlling salary budget and cost, reviewing and approving the employees' wage fixing and salary increase plan, performance appraisal plan and staff benefit plan, etc. (The remuneration of the senior managers of the Company incorporated in the scope of the heads of state-owned enterprises in Jinan shall be determined by the department of SASAC, and the remuneration of other senior managers of the Company shall be determined following the nomination by the Board of Directors and evaluation based on the remuneration appraisal methods formulated by the remuneration appraisal committee and the Board of Directors.)

## 2. Total Annual Remuneration, Beneficiary and Structure Distribution

In 2020, the total remuneration of the Company was RMB615.75 million, with its structure distribution as follows:

RMB209.03 million of fixed compensation, accounting for 33.95% of the total amount; RMB288.87 million of variable compensation, accounting for 46.91% of the total amount; RMB117.85 million of welfare income such as Five Insurance & Two Funds ect., accounting for 19.14% of the total amount.

## 3. The standard of remuneration and performance measurement and risk adjustment

Performance salary of the department/office employees of the Company's head office shall be paid based on appraisal according to the provisions of the Management Measures for Performance Appraisal of

Department/Office of Laishang Bank's Head Office, and that of branch employees based on the provisions of the Management Measures for Performance Appraisal of Laishang Bank.

4. Deferred payment of remunerations and non-cash remunerations, including deduction for some reason.

The Bank has established a deferred payment mechanism for performance salary and implemented deferred payment of performance salary for managers at all levels and employees in positions that have a significant impact on risks, according to different levels of employees and position differences, and set up the deferred payment time and payment ratio and made special provisions on the circumstances of deferred claims and deductions.

## 5. Policy of remuneration

The remuneration system of the Company conforms to the payment concept of the "market-oriented, post-oriented, performance-oriented, capability-oriented" principle. In 2016, the Company carried out the optimization and design program for the remuneration system, implemented a fixed remuneration system on a monthly basis according to the staff's post responsibilities and standards of person-to-job matching, linked the total remuneration to the operating profit throughout the Bank, established a top-down withdrawal mechanism with total remuneration linked to the operating profit as well as bottom-up total remuneration accounting mechanism, and gradually improved the management and control mechanism for total remuneration throughout the Bank.

In accordance with the regulatory requirements and combined with the actual situation, the Company has formulated the Measures for the Administration of Remuneration of Laishang Bank and the Measures for the Administration of Deferred Payment of Performance Remuneration of Laishang Bank, which has been deliberated and adopted by the Company's Staff Congress.

The Company's remuneration adopts uniformly managed and hierarchically implemented management system, and is dedicated to building a scientific and reasonable remuneration management system. The Board of Directors determines the Company's remuneration management system and policy according to the relevant laws and policies of the country, and is ultimately responsible for remuneration management. The Board of Directors sets up the nomination and Remuneration Assessment Committee, and independent director acts as the director of the committee. Senior management is responsible for remuneration management, and the HR Department, Financial Planning Department of the headquarter are responsible for implementing the remuneration policy and Internal Audit Department is responsible for supervising the implementation process.

6. Preparation of annual remuneration plan and completion of assessment

The Company's annual remuneration plan was prepared according to the size of staff, business development, etc. In 2020, various operational indicators of the Company were good, and the remunerations reasonably reflected the performance and risk management of the Company.

7. Exceptions beyond the original remuneration plan

There was no exception beyond the original remuneration plan in the Company during the reporting period.

### II. Profile of Employees

At the end of the reporting period, there were 2,326 employees on duty in the Company, including 2023 with undergraduate or above degree, accounting for 86.97%.

#### 第五章 公司治理结构

#### 一、公司治理情况

报告期内，公司严格遵守《公司法》和《商业银行法》的有关规定，认真落实监管部门关于公司治理的有关规定，致力于进一步完善公司治理结构，增强公司治理机制的有效性，提高董事会决策的科学性。公司治理具体情况如下：

（一）关于股东与股东大会。公司按照《公司章程》和《股东大会议事规则》的要求召集、召开股东大会，积极听取股东的意见和建议，确保股东对公司重大事项的知情权、参与权和表决权。报告期内，公司召开了2019年度股东大会、2020年第一次临时股东大会、2020年第二次临时股东大会，会议严格按照有关规定履行了相关法律程序，保证了股东参会并行使权利，并由律师出具了法律意见书。

（二）关于董事和董事会。公司现有董事9名，其中独立董事3名。董事会能够严格按照《公司章程》的规定，认真履行职责，在股东大会授权下进行决策，强化公司治理，推进战略步伐，促进稳健经营，维护了公司和全体股东的利益。报告期内公司共召开了6次董事会会议。

目前，公司董事会下设1个办公室和6个专门委员会，包括董事会办公室、关联交易控制委员会、风险管理委员会、战略发展委员会、审计委员会、提名与薪酬考核委员会、消费者权益保护委员会，其中风险管理委员会下设声誉风险管理专业委员会和信息科技风险管理专业委员会。6个专门委员会具有明确的职权范围，监管公司不同方面的事务，并对董事会负责，为董事会决策提供了建设性建议，进一步提高了董事会决策的科学性。

（三）关于监事和监事会。公司现有监事9人，其中职工监事3人，外部监事3人。监事会按照《公司章程》的规定认真履行职责，对公司财务、风险管理、资产质量以及部分高级管理人员履职的合规性进行监督检查，定期及不定期地与外部审计机构和管理层进行情况交流，有针对性地开展专项检查，针对风险隐患和发现的问题，向董事会和管理层提出建议和改进意见，从而更加有效地发挥监事会的作用。报告期内公司共召开了5次监事会会议。

（四）关于信息披露与透明度。公司在《金融时报》和公司网站发布了《2019年度报告》，并制作了《莱商银行股份有限公司2019年度报告》（摘要）单行本，及时准确地进行信息披露，切实维护广大股东特别是中小投资者的利益。

#### 二、独立董事及履行职责情况

报告期内，公司董事会现有独立董事3人，独立董事本着为全体股东负责的态度，履行诚信和勤勉义务，认真参加董事会及各专门委员会会议，积极发表意见，维护公司整体利益及中小股东的合法权益不受侵犯。

（一）独立董事参加会议情况。报告期内，独立董事应参加董事会会议18人次，亲自出席18人次，委托出席0人次，缺席0人次。

（二）独立董事对公司有关事项提出异议情况。报告期内，公司独立董事未对公司本年度董事会的议案提出异议。

#### 三、外部监事及履行职责情况

报告期内，公司监事会现有外部监事3人，外部监事本着为全体股东负责的态度，履行诚信和勤勉义务，认真参加监事会及各专门委员会会议，积极发表意见，维护公司整体利益及中小股东的合法权益不受侵犯。

（一）外部监事参加会议情况。报告期内，公司监事应参加监事会会议15人次，亲自出席14人次，委托出席1人次，缺席0人次。

（二）外部监事对公司有关事项提出异议情况。报告期内，公司外部监事未对公司本年度董事会、监事会的议案提出异议。

#### 四、公司经营决策体系

公司最高权力机构是股东大会，通过董事会、监事会进行管理和监督。行长受聘于董事会，对公司日常经营管理全面负责。公司实行一级法人体制，各分支机构均为非独立核算单位，其经营管理活动根据公司授权进行，并对公司负责。

公司无控股股东。公司与大股东在业务、人员、资产、机构、财务五方面完全独立。公司具有独立完整的业务及自主经营能力，董事会、监事会和内部机构能够独立运作。

#### 五、高级管理人员考评及激励机制

报告期内，公司董事会制定了对高级管理人员的绩效考核管理办法，建立了薪酬与公司效益和个人业绩相联系的激励约束机制；考核实行百分制，根据各高管人员的工作性质不同，考核项目有所侧重。对于非执行董事、非职工监事的考核主要以其出席会议次数及履职能力考核为主。制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会董事履职评价制度》，主要分为客观评价、自我评价和相互评价三个部分，根据各项指标具体内容的不同，给予不同的比重。公司制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会对高管人员履职评价办法》《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对董事、监事、高管人员履职评价办法》《关于追究董事、监事及高管人员责任的规定》。公司监事会制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对高管人员履职评价办法实施细则》，明确了监事会对高级管理人员的履职评价，加大了履职问责力度。

# Chapter V Corporate Governance Structure

## I. Corporate governance of the Company

During the reporting period, the Company strictly observed the related provisions in the Company Law and the Law on Commercial Banks, conscientiously implemented the related regulations of the regulatory intuition on corporate governance, and was dedicated to further improving corporate governance structure, enhancing the effectiveness of the corporate governance mechanism and making the decision of the Board of Directors more scientific. The performance of the Company in its corporate governance is as follows:

#### (i) Shareholders and shareholders' general meeting

The Company convened the shareholders' general meeting in accordance with the requirement of the Articles of Association and the Rules of Procedures for Shareholders' Meeting and actively listened to shareholders' opinion and suggestion to ensure that all shareholders have the right to know, to participate and to vote to the major events of the Company. During the reporting period, the Company held its 2019 annual shareholders' general meeting, and 2020 first interim shareholders' general meeting, and 2020 second interim shareholders' general meeting. The meetings went through related legal procedures in strict accordance with the related regulations to ensure that shareholders participate in the meetings and exercise their right. Also, legal opinion was issued by a lawyer.

#### (ii) Directors and the Board of Directors

Currently, the Company has 9 directors, including 3 independent directors. The Board of Directors is able to conscientiously perform its duties in strict accordance with the provisions in the Articles of Association, make decisions with the authorization of the shareholders' general meeting, strengthen the corporate governance, advance strategic steps and promote steady operation which safeguarded the interests of the Company and all shareholders. During the reporting period, the Company held 6 board meetings totally.

Currently, under the Board of Directors of the Company there is 1 office and 6 special committees, including the Office of Board of Directors, Related Transaction Control Committee, Risk Management Committee, Strategic Development Committee, Audit Committee, Nomination and Remuneration Assessment Committee and Consumer Rights Protection Committee. Under the Risk Management Committee are the Special Committee of Reputation Risk Management and Special Committee of IT Risk Management. These 6 special committees have specific scopes of power, supervise and manage the matters at different aspects, and are accountable to the Board of Directors. They provide constructive suggestions for the decision making of the Board of Directors further to improve the science of the decisions made by the Board of Directors.

#### (iii) Supervisors and the Board of Supervisors

The Company has 9 supervisors, including 3 employee supervisors and 3 external supervisors. The Board of Supervisors conscientiously performs its duties in strict accordance with the

provisions in the Articles of Association, carries out supervision and inspection over the compliance of the Company's finance, risk management, asset quality and the duty performance of the senior management, communicates with the external audit units and management regularly or irregularly, conducts targeted special inspections, and puts forward suggestions and improvement opinions to the Board of Directors and the management according to hidden risks or problems discovered, so as to play the role of the Board of Supervisors more effectively. During the reporting period, the Company held 5 board of supervisors meetings totally.

#### (iv) Information disclosure and transparency

The Company published its 2019 Annual Report on Financial Times and its website, and prepared a separate edition of 2019 Annual Report of Laishang Co., Ltd. (Summary), to carry out information disclosure in a timely and accurate manner and safeguard the interests of shareholders, particularly medium and small shareholders.

## II. Independent Directors and Their Performance of Duties

During the reporting period, there were 3 independent directors on the Board of Directors. With an attitude of being responsible to all shareholders, these independent directors performed the obligation of integrity and diligence, earnestly participated in the board meetings and meetings of various special committees, and actively delivered their opinions to safeguard the collective interest of the Company and the legitimate rights and interest of medium and small shareholders.

### (i) Independent directors' presence in meetings

During the reporting period, the number of independent directors attending the board meetings should be 18, with presence in 18 persons, entrusted attendance in 0 persons, and absence in 0 person.

### (ii) Objections of the independent directors to related affairs of the Company

During the reporting period, the independent directors of the Company did not object to any proposal of the Board of Directors of the current year.

## III. External Supervisors and Their Performance of Duties

There were 3 external supervisors in the Board of Supervisors in the Company during the report period. With an attitude of being responsible to all shareholders, these external supervisors performed their duty of integrity and diligence, carefully participated in the meeting of the Board of Supervisors and all special committee meetings, positively gave opinions and prevent overall benefit of the Company and lawful rights and interests of medium and small shareholders from infringement.

### (i) Presence of external supervisors in meetings

During the reporting period, the number of the supervisors attending the Board of Supervisors Meeting should be 15, with presence in 14 persons, entrusted attendance in 1 person and absence in 0 person.

#### (ii) Objection raised by the external supervisors

During the reporting period, the external supervisors of the Company did not raise objections to the proposals of the Board of Directors and Board of Supervisors of the Company of the current year.

## IV. Operation and decision-making system of the Company

The highest authority of the Company is the Shareholders' General Meeting, which is managed and supervised through the Board of Directors and the Board of Supervisors. The president, employed by the Board of Directors, is responsible for all daily operation and management of the Company. Only the head office of the Company is its legal person, and all branches and sub-branches are non-independent accounting units, whose operation and management activities shall be carried out with the authorization of the Company and who shall be responsible for the Company.

The Company has no controlling shareholder. The Company and its majority shareholders are completely independent in terms of business, personnel, assets, organization and finance. The Company has independent and complete business and independent operation ability, and the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors and internal organizations can operate independently.

## V.Assessment and Incentive Mechanism and System of Senior Management

During the reporting period, the Board of Directors of the Company further improved the performance appraisal management methods for senior management, and established an incentive and restraint mechanism which associates remuneration with the Company's effectiveness and personal performance; A hundred-mark system for assessment; the assessment project has an emphasis based on the work nature of senior management. The assessment of non-executive directors and non-employee supervisors has mainly focused on their attendance on meetings and ability to perform their duties. The Evaluation System of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Duty Performance of Directors of the Board of Directors, which includes three parts, i.e., objective evaluation, self-evaluation and mutual evaluation, has been prepared. Different proportions are given according to different contents of various indexes. The Measures of the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Assessment of Senior Management's Performance, the Measures of the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Assessment of Directors, Supervisors and Senior Management's Performance and the Provisions of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Accountability of Directors, Supervisors and Senior Management have been formulated. Meanwhile, the Board of Supervisors further quantified the criteria of assessment, formulated the Implementing Rules of Duty Performance Assessment Method on Senior Management by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., specified the duty performance assessment on senior management by the Board of Supervisors and strengthened the accountability of duty performance.

## VI. Setup of Departments and Branch Organizations

### (i) The Company's Organization Structure Diagram

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_87_459_1036_1290.jpg" alt="Image" width="79%" /></div>


#### (i) The Company's Organization Structure Diagram

### 1. Overview of Hierarchical Management

The Company made planning setup, daily operation management and internal management for branch organizations according to the three-tier organization management system of the headquarter, branch and sub-branch. By the end of the reporting period, The Company had totally 8 branches, 1 business department and up to 106 operating offices.

## 第六章 股东大会情况

报告期内公司共召开了三次股东大会，具体情况如下：

2020年4月25日，在公司综合营业楼一楼礼堂召开了2019年度股东大会，出席会议的股东及股东代理人共48名，代表股份271077.5922万股，占公司总股本的90.35%。大会审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会2019年度工作报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会2019年度工作报告》《关于<莱商银行股份有限公司2019年度审计报告>的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司2019年度财务报告及利润分配方案》《关于<莱商银行股份有限公司2020年度预算>的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司2019年度内部人和股东关联交易执行情况报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2019年度全面风险管理报告》《关于<莱商银行股份有限公司股权托管办法>的议案》《关于莱商银行股份有限公司2020年机构发展规划的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对董事2019年度履职评价报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对监事2019年度履职评价报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对高级管理人员2019年度履职评价报告》《关于对<莱商银行股份有限公司2019年度报告>进行信息披露的议案》《关于对<莱商银行股份有限公司2019年度社会责任报告>进行信息披露的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司关于2020年度拟核销不良贷款额度的议案》。

2020年1月14日，在公司综合营业楼六楼会议室召开了2020年第一次临时股东大会，出席会议的股东及股东代理人共43名，代表股份262175.3452万股，占公司总股本的87.39%。大会审议通过了《关于修改<莱商银行股份有限公司章程>的议案》《关于莱商银行股份有限公司股东大会对董事会授权的议案》《关于增持河南方城凤裕村镇银行有限责任公司股份的议案》《关于增持五台莱商村镇银行股份有限公司股份的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司2019年3季度内部人和股东关联交易执行情况报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2019年3季度风险管理报告》。

2020年12月9日，在公司综合营业楼六楼会议室召开了2020年第二次临时股东大会，出席会议的股东及股东代理人共43名，代表股份271457.49万股，占公司总股本的90.49%。大会审议通过了《关于<莱商银行股份有限公司关联交易管理办法>的议案》《关于<莱商银行股份有限公司资本规划（2021-2023）>的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司关于发行二级资本债券的议案》《关于变更莱商银行股份有限公司名称的议案》《关于修改<莱商银行股份有限公司章程>的议案》《关于段伟董事申请辞职的议案》《关于选举王军为第五届董事会董事的议案》《关于调整2020年度经营目标的议案》《关于追加2020年度拟核销不良贷款额度的议案》《关于续聘中兴华会计师事务所为外部审计机构的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司2020年3季度内部人和股东关联交易执行情况报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2020年3季度风险管理报告》。

### Chapter VI Shareholders' General Meeting

The Company held three general meetings of shareholders during the reporting period, with the details as follows:

On April 25, 2020, the 2019 Annual Shareholders' General Meeting was held in the auditorium on the first floor of the comprehensive business building of the Company. 48 shareholders and authorized agents attended the meeting, representing 2,710,775,922 shares, accounting for 90.35% of the Company's total share capital. The Shareholders' General Meeting has deliberated and adopted the 2019 Annual Work Report of the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Work Report of the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the 2019 Annual Audit Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Financial Report and Profit Distribution Scheme of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the 2020 Annual Budget of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on the Implementation of the Insider and Shareholder-related Transactions of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on Comprehensive Risk Management of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Measures for Equity Trust of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on 2020 Institutional Development Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on Performance Evaluation of Directors by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on Performance Evaluation of Supervisors by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on Performance Evaluation of Senior Management by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Information Disclosure for the 2019 Annual Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Information Disclosure for the 2019 Annual Social Responsibility Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and the Bill on the Amount of Proposed Cancellation of Non-performing Loans in 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.

On January 14, 2020, the First Interim Shareholders' General Meeting of 2020 was held in the meeting room on the six floor of the comprehensive business building of the Company. 43 shareholders and authorized agents attended the meeting, representing 2,621,753,452 shares, accounting for 87.39% of the Company's total share capital. The Shareholders' General Meeting has deliberated and adopted the Bill on Modifying Articles of Association of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the Authorization for the Board of Directors by Shareholders' General Meeting of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Increasing Shares of Henan Fangcheng Fengyu Village Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Increasing Shares of Wutai Laishang Village Bank Co. Ltd., the Report on the Implementation of Insider and Shareholder-related Transactions in the Third Quarter of 2019 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and the Report on Risk Management in the Third Quarter of 2019 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.

On December 9, 2020, the Second Interim Shareholders' General Meeting of 2020 was held in the meeting room on the six floor of the comprehensive business building of the Company. 43 shareholders and authorized agents attended the meeting, representing 2,714,574,900 shares, accounting for 90.49% of the Company's total share capital. The Shareholders' General Meeting has deliberated and adopted the Bill on Management Measures for Related Transactions of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the Capital Plan (2021-2023) of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Issuing Tier 2 Capital Bonds of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Changing the Name of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Modifying Articles of Association of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Application for Resignation of Director Duan Wei, the Bill on the Election of Wang Jun as the Director of the Fifth Board of Directors, the Bill on the Adjustment of Business Objectives in 2020, the Bill on the Amount Addition of the Proposed Cancellation of Non-performing Loans in 2020, the Bill on the Renewal of Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP as an External Audit Institution, the Report on the Implementation of Insider and Shareholder-related Transactions in the Third Quarter of 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and the Report on Risk Management in the Third Quarter of 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.

## 第七章 董事会报告

## 一、2020年度主要经营管理情况

2020年，面对复杂多变的外部环境，公司董事会在广大股东的大力支持和监事会的监督指导下，严格按照《公司法》《商业银行公司治理指引》等法律法规及监管要求，认真勤勉履职，实现了规模、效益、质量的稳步提升。截至2020年12月31日，公司资产总额1540.8亿元，各项存款余额1126.9亿元，各项贷款余额893.8亿元，实现拨备前利润（税前）20.7亿元，不良贷款率较年初下降0.51个百分点，各项业务继续实现高质量稳健发展。

## （一）规范治理机制，持续提升公司治理水平

1. 规范召集召开会议。报告期内，董事会共召开6次会议，审议通过议案72项，对聘任高级管理人员、关联企业授信、资本规划、修改章程等重大事项进行了决策。召集了3次股东大会，审议通过议案33项，对财务决算及预算报告、风险管理报告、董监高人员的履职评价等事项进行了审议。

2.积极发挥各专门委员会的作用。2020年，累计召开专门委员会会议25次，审议通过议案75项。各专门委员会均制定了年度工作计划并依法合规召开会议，持续发挥委员的专业优势，向董事会提供专业意见，切实发挥决策参谋作用，提高了董事会决策的科学性和效率性。

3. 不断加强董事履职能力。2020年，董事勤勉尽职，积极履行董事职责，在会议前认真审阅议案，按时出席会议，在表决重大事项或其他对公司有重大影响的事项时，审慎决策，切实维护了公司和广大投资者的利益。实地到菏泽分行、泰安分行、济南分行、河南方城凤裕村镇银行及部分网点进行调研，详细了解各机构经营发展情况，积极为各机构发展建言献策，为更好地参与公司治理、指导经营管理奠定了良好基础。

4. 完善公司治理制度体系。修订完善《莱商银行股份有限公司关联交易管理办法》，制定《莱商银行股份有限公司股权托管办法》，完善了股权和关联交易管理制度体系，为进一步规范股权及关联交易管理打下了坚实基础。制定《莱商银行股权投资管理办法》，进一步加强对各类被投资机构的管理，逐步完善公司对被投资机构的管理体系。

5. 将党委的核心领导作用有机融入公司治理。根据中国银保监会《健全银行业保险业公司治理三年行动方案》和国资委要求，制定和完善了《莱商银行党委研究决定事项清单、前置研究讨论事项清单及负面清单》，进一步明确了党委前置研究事项范围，切实发挥党委在公司治理中“把方向、管大局、促落实”的作用。

6. 加强股权管理，规范股东行为。2020年，公司向主要股东发函告知银保监会关于股权监管的相关要求、重大违法违规股东名单和案例，强化主要股东的履职履约意识。董事会对主要股东2019年度的履职履约情况进行了专项评估，通过股东信息核查、绘制股权关系图谱等方式，掌握主要股东及其控股股东、实际控制人、关联方、一致行动人、最终受益人等信息，全面梳理股东行为，切

实维护股东权益。

7. 及时准确披露相关信息。报告期内，公司严格按照监管要求，履行信息披露义务，加强信息披露的主动性、针对性和有效性，高质量完成2019年度报告、2020年上半年报告的编制与披露工作。首次披露社会责任报告，从普惠金融、绿色信贷、员工关爱、公益捐助等方面展现了公司的良好品牌形象。相关会议通知和决议均按照规定，在公司网站和《济南日报》及时公告，保证了会议程序的合法性和有效性。

## （二）发挥战略决策职能，引领全行全方位发展

1. 科学制定中长期发展战略。董事会结合《国民经济和社会发展第十四个五年规划和2035年远景目标纲要》内容及公司发展实际，对《莱商银行股份有限公司发展规划（2021-2025）》提出了指导性意见和实施建议，进一步明确了“四步走”战略的实施路径及业务发展的目标，为实现下一个五年宏伟蓝图和实现莱商事业“第二曲线”的新跨越奠定了框架基础。

2. 创新引领业务转型发展。加大金融科技引进运用，努力构建传统业务动能升级、新兴业务全面发力，线上、线下协同服务的发展模式，实现“传统业务+新兴业务”双轮驱动，推动经营业绩全面提升。梳理产品与服务创新的推动、决策和管理机制，规范创新规划、协调和监督流程，完善创新统筹管理与评估框架，创新标准更加明确、流程更加规范高效。不断丰富业务营销与服务手段，降低经营成本，提高工作效率。

3. 加强分支机构和投资机构管理。制定《2020年度机构发展规划》，持续稳步推进机构发展工作，在济南自贸区、济阳开设传统支行，有效提升了公司在济南的市场影响力和品牌知名度。全面梳理公司对村镇银行等被投资单位的管理流程，充分发挥公司作为主发起行和大股东在支持村镇银行发展方面的作用，帮助村镇银行实现稳健可持续发展。

## （三）强化资本管理，提升资本管理精细化水平

1. 完成一级资本补充。按照董事会和股东大会决议，在全国银行间市场成功发行20亿元无固定期限资本债券，优化了公司资本结构，增强了服务实体经济和抵御风险的能力，保障了各项业务稳健增长。

2. 组织实施内部资本充足评估。从治理及政策体系、风险偏好、压力测试、资本规划等方面，充分考虑资产负债规模扩张速度和资本可获得性等因素，扎实开展2019年度内部资本充足评估和压力测试工作，对各类主要风险管理状况、年度风险偏好及风险限额的执行情况、公司面临的各类实质性风险及其对资本充足状况的影响等方面进行评估，确保公司资本充足率持续满足监管要求。

3. 强化资本约束力度。积极贯彻资本精细化管理理念，督促高管层强化资本约束，鼓励发展轻资本型业务，降低高资本消耗型资产占比，切实发挥资本管理对业务的支撑和引导作用。指导高管层合理确定资产规模增速，合理控制表外风险资产增长，确保资本规模与公司经营管理水平相匹配。

## （四）提升风险防控能力，健全合规内控机制

1. 强化全面风险管理体系建设。制定2020年度风险偏好策略，明确全行风险偏好及各类风险管理策略，科学设置资本、收益、风险等3大类33项容忍度指标，使其更加符合公司实际。建立完善

全面风险管理指标监测体系，涵盖信用风险、市场风险等9大类53项风险指标，并设定监测阈值及频率，全面掌握各类风险水平。

2. 持续巩固资产质量攻坚 “334” 战略成果。持续完善不良处置制度体系，修订《莱商银行抵债资产管理办法》等8项制度，进一步夯实资产质量攻坚基础，着力提升不良资产处置质效。通过市场卖断、推进政府支持政策落地、加大司法追偿力度等方式积极拓宽不良资产处置渠道，加快表内外不良资产处置进度。

3. 着力提升内控合规管理水平。以“完善内控管理机制、健全内控管理体系、实现内控措施有效运行”为目标，扎实开展年度内部控制评价工作，完善内部控制体系，不断提升内控管理质效。强化员工行为排查，利用内审系统筛选可疑数据、逐条核实，及时消除各类违规违纪苗头和风险隐患。深化合规建设，组织开展“合规文化深化年”“行业规范建设深化年”活动，持续推进合规制度、合规执行、合规监督、合规整纠等环节建设，努力构建“令行禁止、照章办事、主动合规、知行合一”的合规文化。

4. 规范关联交易管理。根据监管要求，开展信贷系统关联交易模块改造工作，增加关联方管理、关联方限额管理、关联交易管理、查询统计等功能，规范了关联交易的管理流程。对行内关联方、关联关系图谱、金融产品、授信及非授信类关联交易进行梳理，确保关联交易管理及相关指标符合监管要求。

5. 充分发挥内部审计作用。定期听取内部审计工作报告，要求内审部门坚持问题导向、目标导向和结果导向，扎实推进年度审计计划落地，高质量完成各项审计工作。督促高级管理层对外部检查和内部审计发现的问题进行整改，提升信息科技对审计的支撑作用，促进审计在事前风险识别与风险规避方面发挥积极作用，充分发挥“第三道防线”的职能。

## （五）积极履行社会责任，展现良好的企业形象

1. 普惠金融精准发力。制定《莱商银行央行资金复工复产专项贷款操作规程》《疫情防控期间延期还款操作细则》等办法，明确工作标准，细化配套措施，通过扩大无还本续贷范围、增加续贷次数、简化业务流程等措施，全力推动政策落地落实。充分发挥金融主体优势，专门申请国开行1亿元扶贫转贷款，加大扶贫贷款的投放力度。开展“百行进万企”、首贷培植行动等专项活动，以精准的扶持政策助力企业发展。

2. 扎实开展消费者权益保护工作。修订完善《莱商银行个人金融信息保护管理办法》《莱商银行客户投诉管理办法》，提高客户投诉处理和服务效率，提升客户满意度，切实将个人金融信息保护工作落到实处。积极开展“金融知识万里行”“金融知识普及月”“存款保险标识启用”等宣传活动，提高消费者的金融素养，努力践行社会责任。

3.以人为本关爱员工工作生活。打通员工“管理、专业双通道”的职业发展路径，组织开展高级专业技术人员答辩，稳妥、有效完成专业技术人员定级、调薪工作。制定《关心关爱干部开展“向组织说说心里话”专项活动方案》，组织开展谈心谈话活动，加强对干部的关心关爱。建立校招员工双导师工作机制，为每位校招员工指定业务和思想双导师，并定期进行评估和反馈，引导新员工健康、快速成长。开展困难职工帮扶，有效减轻困难职工负担，年内划拨互助金5.5万元，帮扶

困难职工18名。

4. 积极参与社会公益活动。新冠疫情防控期间，对接济南慈善总会，完成首期捐款200万元，并在全行发起抗击疫情捐款倡议，捐款金额28.07万元，以实际行动践行责任与担当。积极参加“莱商行·学子情”希望工程圆梦行动、“春蕾计划”、济南市东西部扶贫协作等工作，共捐助65.8万元，有效提升了公司社会形象。
