## 三、2021年董事会工作计划

## (一) 工作思路

以党的十九大精神和习近平新时代中国特色社会主义思想为指导，按照董事会“一个中心、两个坚持、三个提升、四个加强”的战略部署，高举高质量稳健发展大旗，继续坚持“稳中求进”的总基调，实施“党建统领、改革提质、创新提效、管理提速”四轮驱动，以“对公做精、零售做大、数银起跑、敏捷起步”为着力点，启动“敏捷组织建设年”，全面对标先进、全面转型创新、全面提升能力、全面重塑形象，着力打造客户放心满意的“敏捷银行、快乐银行、清廉银行、价值银行”。

## (二) 工作目标

1. 确保资本充足率达到监管要求；

2. 存款总额达到1254亿元，同比增长127亿元，增幅11.3%，存款偏离度符合监管要求；

3. 贷款总额达到989亿元，同比增长95亿元，增幅10.6%；

4. 实现净利润6.6亿元；

5.不良贷款率控制在2.0%以内，拨备覆盖率在150%以上，拨贷比达到2.5%以上；

6. 实现全年安全营运，无道德案件和重大责任案件发生。

## (三) 工作重点

1. 持续完善公司治理。持续强化党建引领，不断加强各个治理主体之间的有效制衡和协调运作，打造良好的治理文化、完善的治理机制和科学的治理体系。加强董事会建设，强化董事会对股权管理、战略管理、全面风险管理、资本管理等重点领域的最终责任。进一步加强股东、股权及关联方管理，维护股东合法权益。结合银保监会修订的公司治理监管新规，进一步梳理完善公司治理制度体系，确保符合监管规定。

2. 扎实推动战略规划落地实施。稳步推进“四步走”战略实施，加强与相关部门的汇报沟通，密切关注外部舆情与监管动态，确保“四步走”规划平稳有序推进。细化战略规划的实施路径，分区域、分条线、分部门进行目标任务分解，明确战略规划落地实施的责任部门、具体措施和工作方向。建立完善后评估机制，在落地过程中及时纠偏，确保战略落地实施效果。

3. 严守风险防控底线。制定与发展战略相匹配的风险偏好策略，完善风险偏好传导机制及运行监测体系，逐步构建全行统一的风险管理视图。建设更加完善的合规管理架构，丰富合规管理的工具，深入推进涵盖各个风险点的合规管理体系建设，为全行合规经营、规范管理保驾护航。加强资产质量管控，严格客户准入管理，强化风险排查预警，严防不良反弹。

4. 强化资本规划和管理。进一步完善资本管理的顶层设计，推动增资扩股和25亿二级资本债券发行工作，优化资本结构，增强资本实力，支持业务增长和战略规划的实施，实现协调和可持续发展。根据新资本办法的要求，加强对资本计量的监测，增强资本集约化经营能力，提升资本的精细化管理水平。

5. 加快推动数字化转型。优化业务流程，按照“产品获客、社交粘合、数据赋能、分类服务”的总体思路，整合打通营销管理中的各独立环节，建立线上化营销闭环体系。持续推进数据保护和数据治理，完善数据管理体系，提升数据处理能力，强化数据创新应用。提高敏捷化的产品创新能力，通过产品组合、参数化配置、定制化开发等方法，实现产品快速创新，为政府部门、核心企业、中小微企业、商户、自然人提供高效的金融服务。

6. 着力加强提升团队建设。完善新员工招聘培养体系，以完善双导师工作机制、提供展现平台、加强转岗培训为抓手，加强新员工招聘及培训管理。通过社会招聘及时补充各类人才，建立健全各类人才队伍，尤其是加大科技人才招聘力度，实现科技与业务的融合与发展。加强队伍建设，落实全行人才发展战略，做好后备干部、“菁英人才”及内训师管理培训。完善人才激励机制，探索选拔一批具有莱商工匠精神的员工，丰富激励形式，激发员工队伍活力。做好薪酬政策及管理机制的优化调整，探索开展多样化的绩效考核方式，充分发挥绩效考核对业务发展的激励促进作用。

## 四、利润分配方案

2020年度计提盈余公积金58,567,942.07元，计提任意盈余公积金11,713,588.41元，提取一般风险准备8,635,789.56元；剩余未分配利润1,919,872,036.34元结转以后年度。

# Chapter VII Report of the Board of Directors

### I. Review of Main Operation and Management in 2020

In 2020, in the face of a complicated and volatile external environment and under the supervision and guidance of the supervisory authority, the strong support of shareholders, and the supervision and support of the Board of Supervisors, the Board of Directors of the Company, strictly following the Company Law of the People's Republic of China, Guidelines on Corporate Governance of Commercial Banks, and other laws and regulations and supervision requirements, performed its function in a serious and diligent way and promoted the high-quality and steady development of the bank continuously, realizing the coordinated and balanced development in scale, efficiency and quality. As of December 31, 2020, the Company had total assets of RMB154.08 billion, a total deposit balance of RMB112.69 billion, a total loan balance of RMB89.38 billion, a pre-provision profit (before tax) of RMB2.07 billion, and a non-performing loan ratio decreased by 0.51 percentage points compared with the beginning of the year, with the continuous high-quality and steady development of various businesses.

## (i) The Company standardized the governance mechanism and continuously enhanced the corporate governance level

1. The Company convened and held meetings in a standardized manner. During the reporting period, the Board of Directors held 6 meetings in total, with 72 proposals reviewed and approved, in which some major issues such as the appointment of senior managers, the credit of affiliated companies, capital planning, and the amendment of the articles of association were decided. And it convened 3 general meetings of shareholders, with 33 proposals considered and approved, in which matters such as financial final accounts and budget reports, risk management reports, and evaluation of the performance of directors, supervisors and senior managers were reviewed.

2. The Company actively gave play to the role of each special committee. In 2020, the Board of Directors held 25 special committee meetings in total, with 75 proposals reviewed and approved. Each special committee formulated an annual work plan and held meetings in compliance with laws and regulations, with their consistent professional advantages, provided opinions for the Board of Directors and effectively played the role of decision-making consultants, which promoted more scientific and efficient decision-making of the Board of Directors.

3. The Company continued to strengthen the directors' ability to perform their duties. In 2020, the directors performed their duties diligently and actively. They carefully reviewed proposals before meetings and attended on time, and made prudent decisions when voting on major issues or other matters that had a significant impact on the Company, earnestly safeguarding the interests of the Company and investors. The directors went to Heze Branch, Tai'an Branch, and Jinan Branch, Henan Fangcheng Fengyu County Bank, and some branches to conduct investigations on the spot and learned more about the operation and development of each branch. By this, they actively put

forward suggestions for the development of each branch, and laid a solid foundation for them to better participate in corporate governance and guidance on business operation and management.

4. The Company perfected the governance system. The Company, amended the Management Measures for Related Party Transactions of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. and formulated the Measures for Equity Trust of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., which perfected the equity and related-party transaction management system and laid a solid foundation for the next step to standardize the management. And the Company formulated the Management Measures for Equity Investment of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., further strengthening the management of various invested institutions, and gradually improving the management system for those institutions.

5. The company organically integrated the core leading role of the Party committee into the corporate governance. In accordance with the Three-year Action Plan for Improving Corporate Governance of Banking and Insurance Industry by China Banking and Insurance Regulatory Commission and the requirements of the SASAC, the Company formulated and perfected the List of Research Decisions, List of Pre-Research and Discussion Items, and Negative List of the Party Committee of Laishang Bank, which further clarified the scope of Party committee's pre-research items. The role of the Party committee in "controlling the direction, managing the overall situation and promoting the implementation" in corporate governance had been brought into full play.

6. The Company strengthened equity management and regulated shareholders' behavior. In 2020, the Company sent a letter to the major shareholders to inform them the relevant requirements for equity supervision of the China Banking and Insurance Regulatory Commission and the list of shareholders in major violation of laws and regulations and cases, which strengthened the major shareholders' awareness to perform their duties and implement the conventions. The Board of Directors conducted a special assessment of the performance of the major shareholders in 2019. And through the shareholder information verification and drawing equity relationship map, the Board of Directors fully learned about such information as the major shareholders and their controlling shareholders, actual controllers, related parties, persons acting in concert, and ultimate beneficiaries, comprehensively sorted out the behavior of shareholders, and effectively protected their rights and interests.

7. The Company disclosed relevant information in a timely and accurate manner. During the reporting period, the company performed information disclosure obligations strictly following the regulatory requirements. It strengthened the initiative, pertinence and effectiveness of information disclosure, and completed the preparation and disclosure of the 2019 annual report and the 2020 first half report with high quality. The first disclosure of the social responsibility report, in terms of financial inclusion, green credit, employee care, charity donations, etc., reflected the Company's good brand image. The notices and resolutions of the Company's meetings were announced on the Company's website and Jinan Daily in a timely manner in accordance with regulations, ensuring the legality and effectiveness of the meeting procedures.

## (ii) The Company gave play to the function of strategic decision-making and led the all-round development of the bank

1. The Company scientifically formulated the medium and long-term development strategies.

Combining the content of the “Outline of 14th Five-Year Plan for National Economic and Social Development and Long-Term Goals for 2035” with the actual development of the Company, the Board of Directors put forward guiding opinions and implementation suggestions on the Development Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. (2021-2025), further clarified the implementation approach of the "Four Steps" strategy and goal of business development, and laid a framework foundation for realizing the grand blueprint for next five years and realizing the new leap of the "Second Curve" of Laishang Bank's business.

2. The company led business transformation and development through innovation. The company increased the introduction and application of financial technology, strived to build a development mode of upgrading traditional business growth drivers, promoting emerging business fully into action and serving cooperatively online and offline, realized the dual wheel drive of "traditional business + emerging business", and promoted the comprehensive improvement of business performance. And also, the company sorted out the promotion, decision-making and management mechanism of product and service innovation, standardized the planning, coordination and supervision process of innovation, improved the innovation overall management and evaluation framework, and made the innovation standard clearer and the process more standardized and efficient. Meanwhile, the Company constantly enriched business marketing and service means, reduced operating costs and improved work efficiency.

3. The Company strengthened the management of branches and invested institutions. The Company formulated the 2020 Institutional Development Plan, continuously and steadily promoted institutional development, and opened traditional branches in Jinan Free Trade Zone and Jiyang, which effectively increased the Company's market influence and brand awareness in Jinan. The Company comprehensively sorted out the management process to the village banks and other invested institutions, and gave full play to the role of the company as the main initiating bank and major shareholder in supporting the development of the village banks, so as to help the village banks achieve stable and sustainable development.

## (iii) The Company strengthened capital management and enhanced the level of fine capital management

1. The Company completed tier one capital replenishment. In accordance with the resolutions of the Board of Directors and the general meetings of shareholders, the Company successfully issued RMB 2 billion of non-fixed-term capital bonds in the national inter-bank market. The successful issuance of non-fixed-term capital bonds optimized the capital structure, enhanced the ability to serve the real economy and resist risks, and ensured the steady growth of various businesses.

2. The Company organized and implemented the assessment of internal capital adequacy. From the aspects of governance and policy system, risk preference, stress test, capital planning, etc., fully considering factors including scale expansion speed of assets and liabilities and capital availability, the Company solidly carried out the internal capital adequacy assessment and stress test in 2019, and evaluated the main risk management status, annual risk preference and implementation of risk limits, various substantive risks faced by the company and its impact on capital adequacy, so as to ensure that the company's capital adequacy ratio continues to meet regulatory requirements.

3. The Company strengthened capital constraint. The Company actively implemented the fine capital management concept, urged the senior management to strengthen capital constraint, encouraged the development of light capital business, reduced the proportion of assets with high capital consumption, and practically gave play to the roles of support and guidance of capital management for businesses. The Company guided the senior management to reasonably determine the growth rate of the asset scale, reasonably controlled the growth of off-balance-sheet risky assets, and ensured that the capital scale matched the operation and management level of the Company.

## (iv) The Company improved risk prevention and control capabilities and improved compliance and internal control mechanisms

1. The Company strengthened the construction of a comprehensive risk management system. The Company formulated the 2020 risk preference strategy, clarified the risk preference and various risk management strategies of the bank, scientifically set up 33 tolerance indicators in 3 categories of capital, revenue and risk, and made the indicators comply with the actual situation of the Company. The Company established and improved a comprehensive risk management indicator monitoring system, covering 53 risk indicators in 9 categories of credit risk and market risk, and set up monitoring thresholds and frequencies to fully understand various risk levels.

2. The company constantly consolidated the achievements of the “334” strategy for asset quality. The Company continued to improve the non-performing assets disposal system, revised 8 systems including the Measures for the Administration of Debt-expiated Assets in Laishang Bank, further consolidated the foundation for asset quality, and strove to improve the quality and efficiency of non-performing assets disposal. The Company actively expanded the channel of non-performing assets disposal, and accelerated the progress of on and off-balance-sheet non-performing assets disposal by means of market-based outright sale, promoting the implementation of government supporting policies, increasing judicial recovery efforts and etc.

3. The Company strived to enhance the internal control and compliance management level. The Company, taking "the perfection and improvement of the internal control management system and the effective implement of the internal control measures" as orientation, solidly carried out the annual internal control evaluation, improved the internal control system and continued to increase the quality and efficiency of internal control management. The Company strengthened the employee behavior investigation, and used the internal audit system to screen and verify suspicious data one by one to timely eliminate all kinds of violations of regulations and disciplines and potential risks. The Company deepened compliance, organized some activities such as "Compliance Culture Deepening Year" and "Construction of Industry Standards Deepening Year", promoted the construction of compliance regulation, compliance performing, compliance supervision, and compliance rectification and other links, and strove to build a compliance culture of "strict enforcement of orders and prohibitions, action according to the rules, active compliance and unity of knowing and doing".

4. The Company standardized the related party transaction management. According to the regulatory requirements, the Company carried out related party transaction module renovation for the credit management system, increased some functions, such as the management of related parties, limits management of related parties, related party transaction management, and query statistics,

which standardized the related party transaction management procedures. The Company sorted out intra-bank related parties, association spectrum, financial products, and credit and non-credit related party transactions, so as to ensure that the related party transaction management and relevant indicators meet the regulatory requirements.

5. The Company gave full play to the role of internal audit. The Board of Directors regularly listened to the work report on the internal audit. The internal audit department was required to adhere to problem-oriented, goal-oriented and result-oriented approach, push forward the implementation of the annual audit plan, and complete all audit work with high quality. The Board of Directors urged senior management to rectify the problems found in external inspection and internal audit, enhanced the role of Information Technology in supporting audit, promoted audit to play a positive role in pre-risk identification and risk avoidance, and give full play to the function of "the third line of defense".

## (v) The Company fulfilled social responsibility actively and showed a good corporate image

1. The Company made well-targeted efforts in inclusive finance. The Company formulated the Operating Procedures for Special Loan of Laishang Bank from Central Bank Fund for Resumption of Work and Production and the Operation Rules for Deferred Repayment during Epidemic Prevention and Control Period, and clarified working standards, and refined supporting measures. The Company made every effort to promote the implementation of the policy by expanding the scope of non-repayment loan renewal, increasing the number of loan renewal times, simplifying business processes and other measures. The company gave full play to the advantages of financial entities, specially applied for RMB100 million of poverty alleviation loan from China Development Bank, and increased the supply of poverty alleviation loan. Special activities such as "Hundreds of Banks Connecting with Thousands Enterprises" and "first loan support" were carried out to help the development of enterprises with accurate supporting policies.

2. The Company carried out consumer rights protection solidly. The Company revised and improved the Administrative Measures for the Protection of Personal Financial Information of Laishang Bank and the Administrative Measures for Customer Complaints of Laishang Bank, improved customer complaint handling and service efficiency to increase customer satisfaction, and promoted the implementation of personal financial information protection work. The Company actively carried out publicity activities, such as "Mastering Financial Knowledge to Travel around the World", "Financial Knowledge Popularization Month", and "Deposit Insurance Logo Opening Day", to improve financial literacy of consumers and practice social responsibility.

3. The Company put people first and cared for the work and life of employees. The company opened the career development path of "double channels of management and specialty" for employees, organized the defense of senior professional technicians, and steadily and effectively completed the grading and salary adjustment of professional technicians. The Company formulated the Plan to Carry out the Special Activity of Talking to the Organization and Caring for Cadres and organized and carried out heart-to-heart talk activities to strengthen care for cadres. The Company established a dual mentor work mechanism for employees recruited from schools, assigned business and ideological mentors to each employee, and conducted regular evaluation and feedback to guide

the healthy and rapid growth of new employees. The Company carried out the assistance to workers in difficulty to effectively reduce the burden of difficult workers and allocated RMB55,000 of mutual aid funds during the year to help 18 workers.

4. The Company participated in social public welfare activities actively. During COVID-19 prevention and control, the Company donated RMB2 million to Jinan Charity Association in the first installation, and launched a donation initiative to fight the epidemic throughout the bank, with a donation amount of 280,700 Yuan, fulfilling its responsibility with practical actions. The Company actively participated in the "Laishang Action·Student Sentiment", a dream action of the Hope Project, and the "Spring Buds Program", the collaborative work on poverty alleviation in the East and West of Jinan, with a total amount of RMB 658,000, which effectively improved the Company's social image.

## II. Daily Work of the Board of Directors

The Board of Directors of the Company held six general meetings of shareholders during the reporting period, with the details as follows:

On March 12, 2020, the 3rd meeting of the Fifth Board of Directors was held in a communication manner, on which, the Report on Relevant Work During COVID-19 Control and Prevention, the 2019 Annual Work Report on Internal Audit of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Work Report on Consumer Rights Protection of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on the Implementation of Insider and Shareholder-related Transactions of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on Comprehensive Risk Management of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Reporting the Board of Directors for Business Recording in the Fourth Quarter of 2019, the 2019 Annual Report on Self-evaluation of Company Governance of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Measures for Equity Trust of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2020 Risk Preference Strategy of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on Internal Control Evaluation of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on Internal Capital Adequacy Evaluation of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and the 2019 Annual Audit Report on Related Transaction Management of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. were deliberated and adopted.

On April 8, 2020, the 4th meeting of the Fifth Board of Directors was held, on which, the 2019 Annual Work Report of the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Work Report on Operation & Management of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Audit Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Financial Report and Profit Distribution Scheme of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the 2020 Annual Budget of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on the Implementation of Information Technology Work and Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2020 Bill on Information Technology Budget of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on 2020 Institutional Development Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on Performance Evaluation of Directors by the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on Performance Evaluation of Senior Management by the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Information Disclosure for the 2019 Annual Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Information Disclosure for the 2019 Annual Social Responsibility Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the Amount of Proposed Cancellation of Non-performing

Loans in 2020, the Bill on Reporting the Board of Directors for Business Recording in the First Quarter of 2020, the Report on Supervisory Opinions and Rectification in 2019 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Audit Report on Capital Management of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and the Bill on Holding the 2019 Annual General Meetings of Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. were deliberated and adopted.

On June 29, 2020, the 5th meeting of the Fifth Board of Directors was held in a communication manner, on which, the Work Report on Operation & Management in the First Quarter of 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Report on Major Shareholder Evaluation of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Report on the Implementation of Insider and Shareholder-related Transactions in the First Quarter of 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and the Report on Comprehensive Risk Management in the First Quarter of 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. were deliberated and adopted.

On September 24, 2020, the 6th meeting of the Fifth Board of Directors was held, on which, the Work Report on Operation & Management in January-August, 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Management Measures for Related Transactions of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the Three-year Capital Plan (2021-2023) of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Issue of Write-down Tier-2 Capital Bonds by Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Changing the Name of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Preparation for Listing, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Shandong Energy Xinwen Mining Group Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Auction of Shares in Tianjin Wuqing Village Bank Co., Ltd. held by Quan Jian Natural Medicine Science & Technology Development Co., Ltd., the Bill on Reporting the Board of Directors for Business Recording in the Second Quarter of 2020, the Report on the Implementation of Insider and Shareholder-related Transactions in the Second Quarter of 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and the Report on Risk Management in the Second Quarter of 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. were deliberated and adopted.

On November 12, 2020, the 7th meeting of the Fifth Board of Directors was held, on which, the Bill on Application for Resignation of Director Duan Wei, the Bill on Post Dismissal of Vice President Duan Wei of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Nominating Wang Jun as the Director of the Fifth Board of Directors, the Bill on Adjusting the Composition of Staffs in Each Special Committee of the Fifth Board of Directors, the Bill on Modifying Articles of Association of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the Amount Addition of the Proposed Cancellation of Non-performing Loans in 2020, the Bill on the Adjustment of Business Objectives in 2020, the Bill on the Set of Common Benefit Financial Department, the Bill on the Renewal of Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP as an External Audit Institution, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Qilu Transportation Development Group Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Qilu Finance Shandong Economic Development Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Laiwu Iron & Steel Group Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Polaris Bay Group Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Taian Financial Holding Group Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Shandong Energy Xinwen Mining Group Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on the Proposed Addition of Transfer Authorization Limit of Credit Assets to Bank President by the Board of Directors in 2020, the Bill on Reporting the Board of Directors for Business Recording in the Third Quarter of 2020, the

Report on the Implementation of Insider and Shareholder-related Transactions in the Third Quarter of 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Report on Risk Management in the Third Quarter of 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and the Bill on Holding the 2020 Second Interim Shareholders' General Meeting of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. were deliberated and adopted.

On December 29, 2020, the 8th meeting of the Fifth Board of Directors was held, on which, the Bill on Holding Interim Board Meeting and Exempting Notice Period of Meeting, the Bill on Application for Resignation of Director Zhu Ming, the Bill on Post Dismissal of President Zhu Ming of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and the Bill on the Chairman Yue Longjie's Delegation of Performance of President's Duties were deliberated and adopted.

## III. 2020 Work Plan of the Board of Directors

#### (i) Working ideas

Under the guidance of the spirit of the 19th National Congress of the Communist Party of China and Xi Jinping Thought on Socialism with Chinese Characteristics for a New Era, based on the strategic deployment of "one-center, two-insistence, three-upgrade, and four-enhancement" of the Board of Directors, hold the principle of high-quality stable development, keep the overall base of "make progress while ensuring stability", and implement the policy of "guide by Party construction, quality improvement by reform, efficiency promotion by innovation, and speed increase by management". Focusing on "corporation business refinement, retail enlargement, digital bank launching, and agile start", launch the "year for agile organization construction". Follow the example of advanced enterprises, implement the transformation through innovation, improve the capacity, and create a new image comprehensively, and make efforts to develop Laishang into an "agile bank, happy bank, incorruptible bank, and valuable bank" meeting clients' needs.

#### (ii) Work objectives

1. Ensure that the capital adequacy ratio meets the regulatory requirements;

2. Ensure the total deposits to hit RMB125.4 billion, representing an increase of RMB12.7billion or 11.3% on a year-on-year basis, with the deposit deviation complying with the regulatory requirements;

3. The total loans are planned to hit RMB98.9 billion, representing an increase of RMB9.5 billion or 10.6% on a year-on-year basis;

4. The net profit are planned to reach RMB 660 million;

5. The non-performing loan ratio should be controlled within 2.0%, with the provisioning coverage rate above 150% and the ratio of provisioning to loan above 2.5%;

6. Realize safe operation and no moral case and major responsibility case throughout the year.

### (iii) Working priorities

1. Continuously improve corporate governance. Continuously strengthen the Party construction leadership, strengthen the effective checks and balances and coordinated operation among various

governance subjects, and create a good governance culture, perfect governance mechanism and scientific governance system. Strengthen the construction of the Board of Directors, strengthen the final responsibility of the Board of Directors in key fields, such as equity management, strategy management, comprehensive risk management, capital management, etc. Further strengthen the management of shareholders, equities and related parties, and protect the legitimate rights and interests of shareholders. Combined with the new regulation rules of corporate governance revised by the CIRC, further sort out and improve the corporate governance system, to conform with the regulation rules.

2. Solidly promote the implementation of strategy planning. Steadily promote the implementation of the "four-step" strategy, enhance the report and communication with relevant department, focus on external public opinions and regulation trends, to ensure the stable and orderly promotion of the "four-step" strategy. Refine the implementation route of strategy planning, break target tasks down in areas, in lines, and in departments, and clarify the responsible department, specific measures and work direction of the implementation of the strategic planning. Establish and improve post-evaluation system, correct the deviation in the process of implementation, and ensure the implementation effect of the strategy.

3. Strictly adhere to the bottom line of risk prevention and control. Establish a risk preference strategy matching the development strategy, improve the risk preference transmission mechanism and operational and monitoring system, and gradually develop a risk management view applicable to the whole bank. Build a more perfect compliance management framework, enrich the tools of compliance management, and further promote the construction of compliance management system covering all risk points, so as to ensure the whole bank's compliance operating and standardized management. Enhance the management and control of asset quality, strictly conduct client access management, strengthen risk investigation and early-warning, and prevent problems from rebound.

4. Enhance capital planning and management. Further improve the top-level design of capital management, and promote the capital increase and share enlargement and the issue of tier-2 capital bonds worth RMB 2.5 billion. Optimize the capital structure, increase the capital strength, support the implementation of business growth and strategy planning, to realize coordinated and sustainable development. In accordance with the requirements of the new capital approach, strengthen the monitoring of capital measurement, improve the ability of capital-intensive operation, and enhance refined management level of capitals.

5. Accelerate to promote digital transformation. Optimize the business process, according to the general idea of "Obtaining customers with products, connecting customers with social networks, enabling data, and servicing in different ways", integrate and open up all independent links in marketing management, and establish online marketing closed-loop system. Continue to promote data protection and data governance, improve data management system, enhance data processing capacity, and strengthen data innovation and application. Improve flexible product innovation capacity, achieve rapid product innovation through product combination, parametric configuration, customized development and other methods, so as to provide efficient financial services for government departments, core enterprises, small and medium-sized enterprises, individually-owned business and natural persons.

6. Focus on the improvement of team construction. Improve the recruitment and cultivation

system of new employees, and strengthen the recruitment, training, and management of new employees through improving the double-tutor working mechanism, supporting a showing platform, and enhancing the position-transfer training. Timely absorb various talents through social recruitment, establish and improve teams of various talents, especially increase the recruitment of scientific and technological talents, and realize the integration and development of technology and business. Strengthen the team building, implement the talent development strategy of the whole bank, and do a good job in the management training of reserve cadres, "excellent talents" and internal trainers. Improve the talent incentive mechanism, explore and select employees with craftsmanship spirit of Laishang Bank, enrich the incentive forms, and stimulate the vitality of the staff. Improve and adjust salary policy and management mechanism, explore and carry out multiple modes for performance assessment, and bring performance into full play to promote the development of businesses across the bank.

## IV. Profit Distribution Scheme

In 2020, the surplus reserved funds withdrawn were RMB58,567,942.07, the arbitrary reserved funds withdrawn were RMB11,713,588.41 and the general risk reserves allotted were RMB8,635,789.56; the remaining undistributed profit of1,919,872,036.34 is carried over into the later year.

## 第八章 监事会报告

### 一、2020年度监事会召开会议情况

2020年度，公司共召开了5次监事会，具体情况如下：

2020年3月12日，以通讯方式召开第五届监事会第三次会议，审议通过了《关于疫情防控期间相关工作的报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2019年度内审工作报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2019年度消费者权益保护工作报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2019年度内部人和股东关联交易执行情况报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2019年度全面风险管理报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2019年度公司治理自评估报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司股权托管办法》《莱商银行股份有限公司2020年度风险偏好策略》《莱商银行股份有限公司2019年度内部控制评价报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2019年度内部资本充足评估报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2019年度关联交易管理情况的审计报告》。

2020年6月29日，以通讯方式召开第五届监事会第五次会议，审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司2020年1季度经营管理工作报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司主要股东评估报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2020年1季度内部人和股东关联交易执行情况报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2020年1季度全面风险管理报告》。

2020年11月12日，召开第五届监事会第七次会议，审议通过了《关于修改莱商银行股份有限公司章程的议案》《关于追加2020年度拟核销不良贷款额度的议案》《关于调整2020年度经营目标的议案》《关于设立普惠金融部的议案》《关于续聘中兴华会计师事务所为外部审计机构的议案》《关于齐鲁交通发展集团有限公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》《关于齐鲁财金（山东）经济发展有限公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》《关于莱芜钢铁集团有限公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》《关于华创阳安股份有限公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》《关于泰安金融控股集团有限公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》《关于新汶矿业集团有限责任公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》《关于拟追加董事会对行长2020年度信贷资产转让授权额度的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司2020年3季度内部人和股东关联交易执行情况报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2020年3季度风险管理报告》《关于召开莱商银行股份有限公司2020年第二次临时股东大会的议案》。

### 二、监事会就有关事项发表的独立意见

### (一)公司依法经营情况

报告期内，公司坚持依法、合规经营，规范管理，经营决策程序符合法律、法规及公司章程的有关规定，董事及高级管理人员在业务经营及管理过程中谨慎、认真、勤勉，未发现违法违规行为和损害公司、股东利益行为。

### (二) 财务报告真实性

2019年度财务报告已由中兴华会计师事务所根据《企业会计制度》和《企业会计准则》有关规定进行审计，并出具标准无保留意见审计报告。财务报告真实、客观、公允地反映了公司的财务状况和经营成果。

### (三)关联交易情况

公司的关联交易主要是股东贷款。监事会认为，报告期内，公司在处理关联交易时，严格遵守有关规定，履行关联交易操作程序，交易过程体现了公平合理原则，关联交易符合商业原则，

未发现损害股东权益和公司利益的行为。

### (四) 内部控制制度情况

监事会听取和审阅了《公司2019年度内部控制评价报告》，认为公司制定了较为完整、合理、有效的内部控制制度。

### (五)股东大会决议执行情况

监事会对董事会提交股东大会审议的各项报告和提案进行了查阅和调研，没有异议。监事会对股东大会决议的执行情况进行了监督，认为董事会按照公司治理要求，认真执行了股东大会的有关决议。

#### Chapter VIII Report of the Board of Supervisors

## I. Meetings held by the Board of Supervisors in 2020

In 2020, the company held 5 general meetings of the board of supervisors, with the details as follows:

On March 12, 2020, the 3rd meeting of the Fifth Board of Supervisors was held in a communication manner, on which, the Report on Relevant Works During COVID-19 Control and Prevention, the 2019 Annual Work Report on Internal Audit of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Work Report on Consumer Rights Protection of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on the Implementation of Insider and Shareholder-related Transactions of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on Comprehensive Risk Management of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on Self-evaluation of Company Governance of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Measures for Equity Trust of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2020 Risk Preference Strategy of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on Internal Control Evaluation of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on Internal Capital Adequacy Evaluation of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and the 2019 Annual Audit Report on Related Transaction Management of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. were deliberated and adopted.

On April 8, 2020, the 4th meeting of the Fifth Board of Supervisors was held, on which, the 2019 Annual Work Report of the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Work Report on Operation & Management of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Audit Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Financial Report and Profit Distribution Scheme of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the 2020 Annual Budget of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on the Implementation of Information Technology Work and Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Information Technology Budget in 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the Organization Development Plan in 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on Performance Evaluation of Directors by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on Performance Evaluation of Supervisors by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Report on Performance Evaluation of Senior Management by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Information Disclosure for the 2019 Annual Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Bill on Information Disclosure for the 2019 Annual Social Responsibility Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the Proposed Cancellation of Non-performing Loans in 2020, the report on Supervisory Opinion and Rectification in 2019 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2019 Annual Audit Report on Capital Management of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and the Bill on Holding the 2019 Annual General Meetings of Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. were deliberated and adopted.

On June 29, 2020, the 5th meeting of the Fifth Board of Supervisors was held in a communication manner, on which, the Work Report on Operation & Management in the First

Quarter of 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Report on Major Shareholder Evaluation of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Report on the Implementation of Insider and Shareholder-related Transactions in the First Quarter of 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and the Report on Comprehensive Risk Management in the First Quarter of 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. were deliberated and adopted.

On September 24, 2020, the 6th meeting of the Fifth Board of Supervisors was held, on which, the Work Report on Operation & Management in January-August, 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Management Measures for Related Transactions of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the Three-year Development Plan (2020-2022) of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the Three-year Capital Plan (2021-2023) of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Issue of Write-down Tier-2 Capital Bonds by Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Changing the Name of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Preparation for Listing, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Qilu Transportation Development Group Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Xinwen Mining Group Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Auction of Shares in Tianjin Wuqing Village Bank Co., Ltd. held by Quan Jian Natural Medicine Science & Technology Development Co., Ltd., the Bill on Appointing Li Huazhen as President Assistant of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Appointing Chang Yongfeng as President Assistant of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Report on the Implementation of Insider and Shareholder-related Transactions in the Second Quarter of 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and the Report on Risk Management in the Second Quarter of 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. were deliberated and adopted.

On November 12, 2020, the 7th meeting of the Fifth Board of Supervisors was held, on which, the Bill on Modifying Articles of Association of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the Amount Addition of the Proposed Cancellation of Non-performing Loans in 2020, the Bill on Adjustment of 2020 Annual Business Objectives, the Bill on the Set of Common Benefit Financial Department, the Bill on Extending the appointment period of Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP as External Audit Agency, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Qilu Transportation Development Group Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Qilu Finance Shandong Economic Development Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Laiwu Iron & Steel Group Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Polaris Bay Group Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Taian Financial Holding Group Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Xinwen Mining Group Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Proposed Appending of Transfer Authorization Limit of Credit Assets to Bank President by the Board of Directors in 2020, the Report on the Implementation of Insider and Shareholder-related Transactions in the Third Quarter of 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Report on Risk Management in the Third Quarter of 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and the Bill on Holding the Second Interim General Meeting of Shareholders in 2020 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. were deliberated and adopted.

## II. Independent Opinions on Relevant Issues from the Board of

##### Supervisors

##### (i) Legal Operation of the Company

During the reporting period, the Company has stuck to operation in accordance with laws and regulations and normalization of the management. The decision-making procedure met the related provisions of laws, regulations and the Articles of Association. The directors and the senior management were able to conscientiously implement their duties. No director or senior management has been discovered to have breached any laws or regulations or Articles of Association or harmed the interest of the Company when they execute their functions.

##### (ii) Authenticity of Financial Reports

Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP has carefully audited the 2019 Annual Financial Report in accordance with Accounting System for Enterprises and Accounting Standard for Enterprises and issued a standard unqualified audit report. The financial report has truthfully and fairly reflected the Company's financial position and operation results.

##### (iii) The Related Transactions

The related transactions of the Company are mainly shareholders' loans. The Board of Supervisors thinks that during the reporting period when dealing with related transactions, the Company has strictly observed related regulations and performed the operating procedure of related transactions. Transaction processes have reflected the principle of fairness and justifiableness. The related transactions meet the commercial principles and no action compromising the interests of the Company or shareholders has been discovered.

##### (iv) The Internal Control System

The Board of Supervisors heard and deliberated the 2019 Annual Internal Control Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and believed that the Company has formulated the relatively complete, reasonable and effective internal control system.

## (v) Implementation of the Resolutions Approved in the Shareholders' General Meetings

The Board of Supervisors has reviewed and investigated all reports and proposals submitted by the Board of Directors to the Shareholders' General Meeting for deliberation, without any objection. The Board of Supervisors has supervised over the implementation of the resolutions approved at the shareholders' general meetings, and thinks that the Board of Directors of the Company is able to conscientiously implement the related resolutions of the shareholders' general meetings according to the requirements of corporate governance.

### 二、收购及出售资产、吸收合并事项

2020年度无重大收购、出售资产、吸收合并事项。

### 三、公司重大募集资金情况

2020年8月19日，公司发行无固定期限资本债券20亿元。

### 四、公司聘任、解聘会计师事务所情况

2020年，公司聘请中兴华会计师事务所（特殊普通合伙）对公司2019年度会计报表进行了审计。

### 五、重大诉讼、仲裁事项

报告期内，公司无重大诉讼、仲裁事项。截至 2020年12月31日，公司存在诉讼案件633件，公司的诉讼大部分是为收回不良贷款而主动提起的。公司认为，公司诉讼事项不会对公司财务状况或经营成果构成重大不利影响。

### 六、其他重要事项

无。

## II. Acquisition and sell for assets and absorption and merger

There was no significant acquisition or sell for assets or absorption and merger in 2020.

## III. Major Fund Raising of the Company

On August 19, 2020, the company issued write-down non-fixed-term capital bonds worth 2,000,000,000 yuan.

## IV. The Company's Employment and Dismissal of CPAs

In 2020, the Company employed Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP to audit the 2019 financial statements of the Company.

## V. Major Litigation and Arbitration

During the reporting period, there was no major litigation and arbitration in the Company. By December 31, 2020, there were 633 lawsuits in the Company. The major litigation of the Company are actively filed for recovering the non-performing loans. The Company thinks, the major litigation of the Company do not constitute any significant adverse effect on the financial conditions or operating results of the Company.

## VI. Other Important Events

None.
